# Get It Together

Journal: `ep1/quests/main_quest/q303_hands` · `MainQuest` · hash `686544125`.

Journal premise: Your missing uber-netrunner got snared by Dogtown’s head honcho. What a lovely plot twist. Dunno how you wanna get into the Black Sapphire or if it’s even possible, but that’s not the thing I worry about. What does worry me is what you’re gonna do once you get inside. Thoughts?

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 104 exact journal-linked nodes in 13 resources; 14 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 10 content candidate, 4 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

882 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 131 distinct direct resource identities; 13 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
